# 🔍️ Audit: Diff-Only Mutations In Small Plugins (And All Plugins)

Read-only audit against `T/📋️design.md` (laws L1-L5, violation codes). Generated by `T/🗑️generated/audit.py` (static text scan of every `impl ... MutationKind<`, hand `impl ... Mutation<`, `impl ... MutationDiff<`, `impl ... DiffAlgebra<` under `✏️s/🔌️plugins/`). Machine tables: `T/🗑️generated/kinds.tsv`.

## Scope And Count Check

- Handwritten `MutationKind` impls under `✏️s/🔌️plugins/`: **2887** total; **119** in the 14 "small" plugins named in the brief, **2768** in the remaining plugins. The brief's "about 141 kinds" does not match the tree.
- Artifacts (distinct `🗿️artifacts/<name>` roots) with at least one kind: **96**.
- Hand-written `impl ... Mutation<P>` (not `MutationKind`): **80**. Outside `✏️s/🔌️plugins/`: none found (all `MutationKind<`/`Mutation<`/`MutationDiff<` hits under `✏️s` are inside plugins).
- Kinds with **no** L3-relevant test that calls `inverse(` (kind tests or artifact tests): **579**.
- Kinds with a kind-local test calling `absorb(`: **1**. Artifact-level test files calling `absorb(` (sum of inverses): **58** artifacts.

## Method

- Diff body: `fn diff` of the kind, or the delegated `super::diff::diff` in `🔺️diff/🦀️.rs`. Inverse body: `fn inverse`, or `super::inverse::inverse` in `↩️inverse/🦀️.rs`.
- Calls to same-artifact helpers (`fn` in the artifact root, tests excluded) are followed to depth 2 and their hits are attributed to the caller with `[via helper]`.
- Codes: diff `between(` or clone-then-write -> V1-SNAPSHOT-DIFF; generic JSON/value patch names -> V1-GENERIC-DIFF; inverse calling `diff::diff`/`diff(payload, base)`/`between(` -> V2-DIFF-DERIVED-INVERSE; `SetSnapshot`/`Restore*`/`Replace*`/`PatchSnapshot`/generic wrappers -> V2-RESTORE-INVERSE; inverse whose whole body is `Vec::new()`/`vec![]` -> V2-EMPTY-INVERSE; `.apply(`, `&mut <Snapshot-like>`, clone-of-base-then-written in diff/inverse/impl -> V3-LEAF-APPLY; hand `impl Mutation` that applies directly -> V3-HAND-MUTATION; no test calling `inverse(` -> V4-LAW-UNTESTED.
- Limits: text heuristics only (no type checking). `clean` means no V1-V3 code. Each flag still needs the evidence line; V4 is about test presence, not test quality.
